$('.contact--input').on('focus', function () {
  $(this).siblings('.contact--label').addClass('focused');
}).on('blur', function () {
  if ($(this).val().trim() === '') {
    $(this).siblings('.contact--label').removeClass('focused');
  }
});
$(document).ready(function() {
  const $form = $('#book-submit').closest('form');

  // Click on #book-submit triggers form submit
  $('#book-submit').on('click', function(e) {
    e.preventDefault();
    $form.submit();
  });

  // Pressing Enter inside any input/textarea within the form submits it
  $form.on('keydown', function(e) {
    if (e.key === 'Enter' && !$(e.target).is('textarea')) {
      e.preventDefault(); // prevent double submit or line break
      $form.submit();
    }
  });
});

document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", function () {
  // ------------------------------
  // 1) Label focus/blur animation (requires jQuery)
  // ------------------------------
  if (window.jQuery) {
    $('.contact--input').on('focus', function () {
      $(this).siblings('.contact--label').addClass('focused');
    }).on('blur', function () {
      if ($(this).val().trim() === '') {
        $(this).siblings('.contact--label').removeClass('focused');
      }
    });
  }

  // ------------------------------
  // 2) HubSpot hidden fields
  // ------------------------------
  function setHiddenValue(id, value) {
    const el = document.getElementById(id);
    if (el) el.value = value || '';
  }

  function getHubspotUTK() {
    const name = 'hubspotutk=';
    const decodedCookie = decodeURIComponent(document.cookie || '');
    const ca = decodedCookie.split(';');
    for (let i = 0; i < ca.length; i++) {
      const c = ca[i].trim();
      if (c.indexOf(name) === 0) return c.substring(name.length);
    }
    return '';
  }

  setHiddenValue('hutk', getHubspotUTK());
  setHiddenValue('pageUri', window.location.href);
  setHiddenValue('pageId', document.body.getAttribute('data-wf-page') || '');
  setHiddenValue('pageName', document.title);

  fetch('https://api.ipify.org?format=json')
    .then(res => res.json())
    .then(data => setHiddenValue('ipAddress', data.ip))
    .catch(err => console.error('IP fetch error', err));

solutions--faq-grid"><div><h2 class="solutions--faq-header">FAQ</h2></div><div><div class="trends--faq bg-dark"><div data-w-id="1f31c2a9-954a-2ae3-e7a7-fad0c48810bd" class="trends--faq-toggle bg-dark"><div class="trends--faq-question-wrapper bg-dark"><div class="trends--faq-question bg-dark">How can AI drive real value in my enterprise beyond pilots and proofs of concept?</div><div id="w-node-_1f31c2a9-954a-2ae3-e7a7-fad0c48810c0-a754de27" class="trends--faq-toggle-icon bg-dark"><svg id="w-node-_1f31c2a9-954a-2ae3-e7a7-fad0c48810c1-a754de27" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="100%" viewBox="0 0 16 16" fill="none" class="icon--plus bg-dark"><path d="M8.66797 1.27905V14.7212H7.33203V1.27905H8.66797Z" fill="currentColor" class="vertical bg-dark" style="display: block;"></path><path d="M14.7212 7.33203V8.66797H1.27905V7.33203H14.7212Z" fill="currentColor" class="horizontal bg-dark"></path></svg></div></div><div class="trends--faq-answer-wrapper bg-dark" style="height: 0px;"><div class="trends--faq-answer">AI delivers impact when it’s embedded in core workflows, runs on your real operational data, and is governed by transparent metrics and SLAs, not just deployed for automation’s sake or as experimental pilots. With Invisible’s modular platform, you plug in only the pieces you need (data, agents, humans-in-the-loop, evaluations), and drive outcomes you can measure, fast.</div></div></div></div><div class="trends--faq bg-dark"><div data-w-id="049a544f-68d4-8362-16d7-b90b498786d2" class="trends--faq-toggle bg-dark"><div class="trends--faq-question-wrapper bg-dark"><div class="trends--faq-question bg-dark">What are the most common roadblocks to scaling AI in large organizations?</div><div id="w-node-_049a544f-68d4-8362-16d7-b90b498786d6-a754de27" class="trends--faq-toggle-icon bg-dark"><svg id="w-node-_049a544f-68d4-8362-16d7-b90b498786d7-a754de27" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="100%" viewBox="0 0 16 16" fill="none" class="icon--plus bg-dark"><path d="M8.66797 1.27905V14.7212H7.33203V1.27905H8.66797Z" fill="currentColor" class="vertical bg-dark" style="display: block;"></path><path d="M14.7212 7.33203V8.66797H1.27905V7.33203H14.7212Z" fill="currentColor" class="horizontal bg-dark"></path></svg></div></div><div class="trends--faq-answer-wrapper bg-dark" style="height: 0px;"><div class="trends--faq-answer">Typical barriers include fragmented or unstructured data, challenges with legacy system integration, talent and skills gaps, lack of internal AI expertise, and uncertainty about how to measure ROI for large AI projects.</div></div></div></div><div class="trends--faq bg-dark"><div data-w-id="a78f4f69-87aa-e116-c6d5-966f9e265d33" class="trends--faq-toggle bg-dark"><div class="trends--faq-question-wrapper bg-dark"><div class="trends--faq-question bg-dark">How do we measure and sustain AI’s long-term business impact?</div><div id="w-node-a78f4f69-87aa-e116-c6d5-966f9e265d37-a754de27" class="trends--faq-toggle-icon bg-dark"><svg id="w-node-a78f4f69-87aa-e116-c6d5-966f9e265d38-a754de27" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="100%" viewBox="0 0 16 16" fill="none" class="icon--plus bg-dark"><path d="M8.66797 1.27905V14.7212H7.33203V1.27905H8.66797Z" fill="currentColor" class="vertical bg-dark" style="display: block;"></path><path d="M14.7212 7.33203V8.66797H1.27905V7.33203H14.7212Z" fill="currentColor" class="horizontal bg-dark"></path></svg></div></div><div class="trends--faq-answer-wrapper bg-dark" style="height: 0px;"><div class="trends--faq-answer">Begin with a workflow that will drive the most impact and set clear business goals. Track a few simple metrics (time saved, cost reduced, revenue or quality gains) on a monthly/quarterly review, and keep scaling what moves the numbers while fixing or retiring what doesn’t, with a named owner to keep it on track.</div></div></div></div><div class="trends--faq bg-dark"><div data-w-id="c3ee221b-29ef-802a-5aee-d4889e0efbbf" class="trends--faq-toggle bg-dark"><div class="trends--faq-question-wrapper bg-dark"><div class="trends--faq-question bg-dark">Why do many enterprise AI projects struggle or fail to reach full deployment?</div><div id="w-node-c3ee221b-29ef-802a-5aee-d4889e0efbc3-a754de27" class="trends--faq-toggle-icon bg-dark"><svg id="w-node-c3ee221b-29ef-802a-5aee-d4889e0efbc4-a754de27" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="100%" viewBox="0 0 16 16" fill="none" class="icon--plus bg-dark"><path d="M8.66797 1.27905V14.7212H7.33203V1.27905H8.66797Z" fill="currentColor" class="vertical bg-dark" style="display: block;"></path><path d="M14.7212 7.33203V8.66797H1.27905V7.33203H14.7212Z" fill="currentColor" class="horizontal bg-dark"></path></svg></div></div><div class="trends--faq-answer-wrapper bg-dark" style="height: 0px;"><div class="trends--faq-answer">AI initiatives often stall due to shallow or siloed data, poor alignment with business strategy, insufficient change management, and a lack of clear governance, resulting in high pilot rates but low operational adoption.k.</div></div></div></div><div class="trends--faq bg-dark"><div data-w-id="b450faeb-ec03-e2ea-9223-dec6a45a9ee4" class="trends--faq-toggle bg-dark"><div class="trends--faq-question-wrapper bg-dark"><div class="trends--faq-question bg-dark">How can I make a general AI model deliver impact in my operations?</div><div id="w-node-b450faeb-ec03-e2ea-9223-dec6a45a9ee8-a754de27" class="trends--faq-toggle-icon bg-dark"><svg id="w-node-b450faeb-ec03-e2ea-9223-dec6a45a9ee9-a754de27" // -----------------------------
// SVG Rendering Logic
// -----------------------------
$(document).ready(function () {
  document.querySelectorAll('.cms-svg').forEach(wrapper => {
    const raw = wrapper.textContent.trim();
    if (raw) wrapper.innerHTML = raw;
  });
});

// -----------------------------
// Desktop Navigation Logic
// -----------------------------

$(document).ready(function () {
  const ACTIVE_CLASS = 'nav--dd-active';
  const NAV_OPEN_CLASS = 'is--open';
  const $wrapper = $('.nav--dd-list-full-wrapper');
  const $menus = $('.nav--dd-list-full');
  const $navMain = $('.nav');

  let closeTimer;

  function isDesktop() {
    return window.innerWidth >= 992;
  }

  function updateNav(isOpen, targetHeight = 0) {
    requestAnimationFrame(() => {
      if (isOpen) {
        clearTimeout(closeTimer);
        $navMain.addClass(NAV_OPEN_CLASS);
        $wrapper.css('height', targetHeight + 'px');
      } else {
        $navMain.removeClass(NAV_OPEN_CLASS);
        $wrapper.css('height', '0px');
        $('.nav--dd').removeClass(ACTIVE_CLASS);
      }
    });
  }

  $('.nav--dd, .nav--dd-list-full-wrapper').on('mouseenter', function () {
    if (!isDesktop()) return;

    clearTimeout(closeTimer);

    const $this = $(this).hasClass('nav--dd') ? $(this) : $('.nav--dd.' + ACTIVE_CLASS);
    const key = $this.attr('ddmenu');
    if (!key) return;

    const $target = $menus.filter('[ddmenu="' + key + '"]');
    if (!$target.length) return;

    $('.nav--dd').not($this).removeClass(ACTIVE_CLASS);
    $this.addClass(ACTIVE_CLASS);

    $menus.hide();
    $target.show();

    updateNav(true, $target.outerHeight());
  });

  $('.nav--dd, .nav--dd-list-full-wrapper').on('mouseleave', function () {
    if (!isDesktop()) return;

    closeTimer = setTimeout(() => updateNav(false), 200);
  });

  $(window).on('resize', function () {
    if (!isDesktop()) updateNav(false);
  });
});


// -----------------------------
// Desktop Navigation Logic
// -----------------------------
/* new code
$(document).ready(function () {
  const ACTIVE_CLASS = 'nav--dd-active';
  const NAV_OPEN_CLASS = 'is--open';
  const DIMMED_CLASS = 'is--dimmed';
  
  const $wrapper = $('.nav--dd-list-full-wrapper');
  const $menus = $('.nav--dd-list-full');
  const $navMain = $('.nav');
  const $allNavItems = $('.nav--dd, .nav--link'); // Unified selector

  let closeTimer;

  function isDesktop() {
    return window.innerWidth >= 992;
  }

  // updateNav now strictly handles the dropdown size/state, not the dimming
  function updateNav(isOpen, targetHeight = 0) {
    requestAnimationFrame(() => {
      if (isOpen) {
        $navMain.addClass(NAV_OPEN_CLASS);
        $wrapper.css('height', targetHeight + 'px');
      } else {
        $navMain.removeClass(NAV_OPEN_CLASS);
        $wrapper.css('height', '0px');
        $('.nav--dd').removeClass(ACTIVE_CLASS);
      }
    });
  }

  // 1. Handle hovering over ANY top-level link (dropdowns AND standard links)
  $allNavItems.on('mouseenter', function () {
    if (!isDesktop()) return;

    // Stop the menu from closing/resetting if we are moving between links
    clearTimeout(closeTimer);

    // Dim all items except the one currently being hovered
    $allNavItems.not(this).addClass(DIMMED_CLASS);
    $(this).removeClass(DIMMED_CLASS);

    // If it's a dropdown trigger, open its specific menu
    if ($(this).hasClass('nav--dd')) {
      const key = $(this).attr('ddmenu');
      if (!key) return;

      const $target = $menus.filter('[ddmenu="' + key + '"]');
      if (!$target.length) return;

      $('.nav--dd').removeClass(ACTIVE_CLASS);
      $(this).addClass(ACTIVE_CLASS);

      $menus.hide();
      $target.show();

      updateNav(true, $target.outerHeight());
    } else {
      // If it's a standard .nav--link, ensure any open dropdowns close
      updateNav(false);
    }
  });

  // 2. Handle hovering inside the open dropdown wrapper itself
  $wrapper.on('mouseenter', function () {
    if (!isDesktop()) return;
    clearTimeout(closeTimer);
    
    // Ensure the active dropdown trigger stays highlighted, dim everything else
    const $activeTrigger = $('.nav--dd.' + ACTIVE_CLASS);
    $allNavItems.not($activeTrigger).addClass(DIMMED_CLASS);
    $activeTrigger.removeClass(DIMMED_CLASS);
  });

  // 3. Handle mouse leaving the navigation area completely
  $('.nav--dd, .nav--link, .nav--dd-list-full-wrapper').on('mouseleave', function () {
    if (!isDesktop()) return;

    // Start timer to close menu and remove all dimmed states
    closeTimer = setTimeout(() => {
      updateNav(false);
      $allNavItems.removeClass(DIMMED_CLASS);
    }, 200);
  });

  // 4. Handle resizing the window
  $(window).on('resize', function () {
    if (!isDesktop()) {
      updateNav(false);
      $allNavItems.removeClass(DIMMED_CLASS);
    }
  });
});

// -----------------------------
// Mobile Navigation Logic
// -----------------------------
$(document).ready(function () {
  $('.nav--mobile--top-level-new .nav--link').on('click', function (e) {
    if (window.innerWidth <= 991) {
      e.preventDefault();
      const linkName = $(this).attr('link-name');
      $('.nav--mobile--child-level').show();
      $('.nav--mobile--child-level > div').hide();
      $('.nav--mobile--' + linkName).show();
    }
  });

  $('.nav--link.mobile--back').on('click', function () {
    $('.nav--mobile--child-level, .nav--mobile--child-level > div').hide();
  });
});

// -----------------------------
// Announcement Banner Logic
// -----------------------------
$(document).ready(function () {
  const mobileBreakpoint = 991;
  const activeClass = 'has--bar';

  function toggleMobileNavClass() {
    if ($(window).width() <= mobileBreakpoint) {
      const $banner = $('.announcement-banner--wrapper');
      const $nav = $('.nav--mobile-links-wrapper');
      $banner.length && $banner.is(':visible')
        ? $nav.addClass(activeClass)
        : $nav.removeClass(activeClass);
    } else {
      $('.nav--mobile-links-wrapper').removeClass(activeClass);
    }
  }

  toggleMobileNavClass();

  let resizeTimer;
  $(window).on('resize', function () {
    clearTimeout(resizeTimer);
    resizeTimer = setTimeout(toggleMobileNavClass, 250);
  });
});

// -----------------------------
// HubSpot Hidden Fields
// -----------------------------
function setHiddenValue(id, value) {
  const el = document.getElementById(id);
  if (el) el.value = value || '';
}

function getHubspotUTK() {
  const name = 'hubspotutk=';
  const decodedCookie = decodeURIComponent(document.cookie || '');
  return decodedCookie.split(';').map(c => c.trim()).find(c => c.startsWith(name))?.substring(name.length) || '';
}

requestIdleCallback(() => {
  setHiddenValue('hutk', getHubspotUTK());
  setHiddenValue('pageUri', window.location.href);
  setHiddenValue('pageId', document.body.getAttribute('data-wf-page') || '');
  setHiddenValue('pageName', document.title);

  setTimeout(() => {
    fetch('https://api.ipify.org?format=json')
      .then(res => res.json())
      .then(data => setHiddenValue('ipAddress', data.ip))
      .catch(err => console.error('IP fetch error', err));
  }, 3000);
});
</script>

<script>
$(document).ready(function() {

$('#txt-and-more').appendTo('.hp--solutions-tabs-menu');
function updateTabColors() {
  // 1. Reset: Remove any previous 'data-color' styling from all icons
  $('.hp--platform-tabs-link-icon').css('color', '');

  // 2. Find the Active Tab: Select the parent link that is currently active
  const $activeTab = $('.hp--platform-tabs-link.w--current');

  // 3. Get the Icon: Select the child icon within the active tab
  const $activeIcon = $activeTab.find('.hp--platform-tabs-link-icon');

  // 4. Get the Color Value: Extract the data-color attribute
  const colorValue = $activeIcon.attr('data-color');

  // 5. Apply the Color: Set the CSS 'color' property on the icon
  if (colorValue) {
    $activeIcon.css('color', colorValue);
  }
}

// 6. Run the function on document ready to apply the initial color
$(document).ready(function() {
  updateTabColors();

  // 7. Event Listener: If your tabs change based on user interaction (like a click),
  // you must call this function again. Adjust the selector to your tab container.
  $('.hp--platform-tabs-link').on('click', function() {
    // A small delay might be needed if the class change happens *after* the click event
    setTimeout(updateTabColors, 10);
  });
});

$(document).ready(function() {

    // --- Modal Closing Function (Reusable) ---
    function closeModal() {
        // 1. Hide the main modal wrapper
        $('.hp--platform-modal-wrapper').css('display', 'none');

        // 2. Hide all modal item elements
        $('.hp--platform-modal-item').css('display', 'none');

        // 3. Re-enable scrolling on the main body
        $('body').css('overflow', '');
    }

    // -----------------------------------------------------------------
    // --- 1. MODAL OPENING LOGIC (Triggers on icon click) ---
    // -----------------------------------------------------------------
    $('.hp--platform-tabs-pane-icon, .hp--platform-mobile-open').on('click', function() {
        var targetItemId = $(this).attr('data-item');

        // Show the correct content and the wrapper
        $('.hp--platform-modal-item').css('display', 'none');
        $('#' + targetItemId).css('display', 'block');
        $('.hp--platform-modal-wrapper').css('display', 'block');

        // Lock the body scroll
        $('body').css('overflow', 'hidden');
    });

    // -----------------------------------------------------------------
    // --- 2. MODAL CLOSING TRIGGERS ---
    // -----------------------------------------------------------------

    // Trigger A: Close Button Click (.hp--platform-modal-close)
    $('.hp--platform-modal-close').on('click', function() {
        closeModal();
    });

    // Trigger B: Outside Click (Clicking the Wrapper .hp--platform-modal-wrapper)
    $('.hp--platform-modal-wrapper').on('click', function(event) {
        // Only close if the click was directly on the wrapper, not the content inside
        if ($(event.target).is('.hp--platform-modal-wrapper')) {
            closeModal();
        }
    });

    // Trigger C: Keyboard Press (ESC Key)
    $(document).on('keydown', function(event) {
        // Check if the key pressed is the Escape key (keyCode 27)
        if (event.key === 'Escape' || event.keyCode === 27) {
            // Check if the modal is currently visible before closing
            if ($('.hp--platform-modal-wrapper').css('display') === 'block') {
                closeModal();
            }
        }
    });

});

$(document).ready(function() {
    
    // --- Configuration ---
    const mobileBreakpoint = 991;
    const fixedActiveColor = '#0C76FE'; 
    const mediaQuery = window.matchMedia(`(max-width: ${mobileBreakpoint}px)`);
    
    // --- Selectors for both accordion groups ---
    const platformRowSelector = '.hp--platform-mobile-row';
    const solutionsRowSelector = '.hp--solutions-mobile-row';
    const platformContentSelector = '.hp--platform-mobile-content-wrapper';
    const solutionsContentSelector = '.hp--solutions-mobile-content-wrapper';
    
    // Specific icon selectors
    const platformIconSelector = '.hp--platform-tabs-link-icon'; // Icon for data-color logic
    const solutionsIconSelector = '.hp--solutions-tabs-link-icon'; // Icon for fixed color logic
    
    const $allRows = $(platformRowSelector + ', ' + solutionsRowSelector);

    // Find the first instance of each type for initial opening
    const $firstPlatformRow = $(platformRowSelector).first();
    const $firstSolutionsRow = $(solutionsRowSelector).first(); 

    /**
     * Finds the correct icon selector for a given row.
     */
    function getIconSelector($rowElement) {
        if ($rowElement.is(platformRowSelector)) {
            return platformIconSelector;
        }
        if ($rowElement.is(solutionsRowSelector)) {
            return solutionsIconSelector;
        }
        return null;
    }

    /**
     * Determines the active color based on the row's configuration and applies it.
     */
    function applyIconColor($rowElement) {
        const iconSelector = getIconSelector($rowElement);
        if (!iconSelector) return;
        
        const $icon = $rowElement.find(iconSelector);
        let color = null;

        if ($rowElement.is(platformRowSelector)) {
            // Platform Accordion (Custom data-color)
            color = $icon.attr('data-color');
        } else if ($rowElement.is(solutionsRowSelector)) {
            // Solutions Accordion (Fixed Blue Color)
            color = fixedActiveColor;
        }

        if (color) {
            $icon.css('color', color);
        }
    }

    /**
     * Removes the color from the icon.
     */
    function removeIconColor($rowElement) {
        const iconSelector = getIconSelector($rowElement);
        if (iconSelector) {
            $rowElement.find(iconSelector).css('color', ''); 
        }
    }

    // Helper to get the correct content selector for a given row
    function getContentSelectorString($rowElement) {
        if ($rowElement.is(platformRowSelector)) {
            return platformContentSelector;
        }
        if ($rowElement.is(solutionsRowSelector)) {
            return solutionsContentSelector;
        }
        return null;
    }

    // 1. Initial setup: Open the first of EACH type on mobile load
    function initializeAccordionState() {
        if (mediaQuery.matches) {
            
            // A. Hide ALL content wrappers and reset states/colors
            $allRows.each(function() {
                const $row = $(this);
                $row.removeClass('active');
                
                $row.find(platformContentSelector + ', ' + solutionsContentSelector)
                    .removeClass('active')
                    .hide();
                    
                removeIconColor($row);
            });
            
            // B. Open the first Platform row
            if ($firstPlatformRow.length) {
                $firstPlatformRow.addClass('active');
                $firstPlatformRow.find(platformContentSelector).addClass('active').show();
                applyIconColor($firstPlatformRow);
            }
            
            // C. Open the first Solutions row
            if ($firstSolutionsRow.length) {
                $firstSolutionsRow.addClass('active');
                $firstSolutionsRow.find(solutionsContentSelector).addClass('active').show();
                applyIconColor($firstSolutionsRow);
            }
            
        } else {
            // Desktop (> 991px): Ensure all content is visible, and all custom colors/classes are gone
            $allRows.each(function() {
                const $row = $(this);
                $row.removeClass('active');
                $row.find(platformContentSelector + ', ' + solutionsContentSelector)
                    .removeClass('active')
                    .show();
                removeIconColor($row); 
            });
        }
    }

    // Run initialization on load
    initializeAccordionState();

    // 2. Unified click handler 
    $(platformRowSelector + ', ' + solutionsRowSelector).on('click', function(e) {
        
        if (!mediaQuery.matches) {
            return;
        }
        
        const $clickedRow = $(this);
        const isActive = $clickedRow.hasClass('active');
        
        const contentSelectorString = getContentSelectorString($clickedRow);
        const $contentWrapper = $clickedRow.find(contentSelectorString).first();

        // --- Prevent self-collapse (Requirement) ---
        if (isActive) {
            return;
        }

        // --- Close other active rows *of the same type* (Requirement) ---
        let $rowsToClose = [];
        let rowSelectorToClose = $clickedRow.is(platformRowSelector) ? platformRowSelector : solutionsRowSelector;
        
        // Find all active rows that share the same class as the clicked row
        $(rowSelectorToClose + '.active').each(function() {
            $rowsToClose.push($(this));
        });

        $rowsToClose.forEach(function($otherRow) {
            const otherContentSelectorString = getContentSelectorString($otherRow);
            const $otherContent = $otherRow.find(otherContentSelectorString).first();
            
            $otherRow.removeClass('active');
            $otherContent.removeClass('active').slideUp(300);
            removeIconColor($otherRow); 
        });


        // --- Open the newly clicked accordion ---
        $clickedRow.addClass('active');
        $contentWrapper.addClass('active').slideDown(300);
        applyIconColor($clickedRow); 
        
    });

    // 3. Handle window resize event
    mediaQuery.addListener(function(mql) {
        initializeAccordionState();
    });
});

</script>

$(document).ready(function() {
  const $form = $('#book-submit').closest('form');

  // Click on #book-submit triggers form submit
  $('#book-submit').on('click', function(e) {
    e.preventDefault();
    $form.submit();
  });

  // Pressing Enter inside any input/textarea within the form submits it
  $form.on('keydown', function(e) {
    if (e.key === 'Enter' && !$(e.target).is('textarea')) {
      e.preventDefault(); // prevent double submit or line break
      $form.submit();
    }
  });
});

<script>
const FADE_DURATION = 300;

// Build a sorted array of words based on CMS "Order"
function getSortedWords() {
    const slides = document.querySelectorAll('.hp--swiper .swiper-slide');
    const items = [];

    slides.forEach(slide => {
        const word = slide.getAttribute('data-word');
        const order = parseInt(slide.getAttribute('data-order'), 10) || 0;
        items.push({ word, order });
    });

    // Sort by CMS order
    items.sort((a, b) => a.order - b.order);

    // Return only the words in sorted order
    return items.map(item => item.word);
}

const sortedWords = getSortedWords();

// Update headline text based on sorted order, not slide order
function updateHeadline(swiperInstance) {
    const index = swiperInstance.realIndex % sortedWords.length;
    const newText = sortedWords[index];

    const $headline = $('.hp--swiper--headline-word');

    $headline.fadeOut(FADE_DURATION, function() {
        $(this).text(newText).fadeIn(FADE_DURATION);
    });
}

// Swiper initialization
var swiper = new Swiper(".hp--swiper", {
    centeredSlides: true,
    spaceBetween: 24,
    initialSlide: 0,
    loop: true,
    autoplay: { enabled: true, pauseOnMouseEnter: true },
    breakpoints: {
        768: { slidesPerView: 2 },
        1024: { slidesPerView: 3 },
        1280: { slidesPerView: 3 },
        1920: { slidesPerView: 3 }
    },

    on: {
        init: function() {
            $('.hp--swiper--headline-word').text(sortedWords[0]);
        },
        slideChangeTransitionStart: function() {
            updateHeadline(this);
        }
    }
});
</script>

<script>
document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", () => {
  const firstLoop = [
    { text: "frontline enterprise", time: 1600 },
    { text: "professional sports", time: 3700 },
    { text: "insurance", time: 3867 },
    { text: "healthcare", time: 3700 },
    { text: "supply chains", time: 3633 },
    { text: "public sector", time: 3500 }
  ];

  const looped = [
    { text: "frontline enterprise", time: 2610 },
    { text: "professional sports", time: 3700 },
    { text: "insurance", time: 3867 },
    { text: "healthcare", time: 3700 },
    { text: "supply chains", time: 3633 },
    { text: "public sector", time: 3500 }
  ];

  let words = firstLoop;
  let index = 0;
  let hasLoopedOnce = false;
  const container = document.getElementById("cycler");

  function setWord(wordIndex) {
    const wordData = words[wordIndex];
    if (!wordData) return;
    
    container.innerHTML = "";
    const isMobile = window.innerWidth <= 479;
    const lottieClassName = wordData.text.replace(/\s+/g, '-');

    // 1. Manage Lottie Playback (The Verified Method)
    document.querySelectorAll(".hp--lottie").forEach(player => {
      if (player.classList.contains(lottieClassName)) {
        player.classList.add("active");
        // Ensure looping is active and play
        if (player.setLooping) player.setLooping(true);
        player.play();
      } else {
        player.classList.remove("active");
        player.pause();
      }
    });

    // 2. Build Animated Text
    const wordParts = wordData.text.split(" ");
    let letterIndex = 0;
    wordParts.forEach((word, wIndex) => {
      const wordSpan = document.createElement("span");
      wordSpan.className = "word";
      word.split("").forEach((char) => {
        const letterSpan = document.createElement("span");
        letterSpan.className = "letter";
        letterSpan.textContent = char;
        letterSpan.style.animationDelay = `${letterIndex * 0.01}s`;
        letterIndex++;
        wordSpan.appendChild(letterSpan);
      });
      container.appendChild(wordSpan);
      if (wIndex < wordParts.length - 1) {
        const spaceSpan = document.createElement("span");
        spaceSpan.className = "word-space";
        spaceSpan.textContent = isMobile ? " " : "\u00A0";
        container.appendChild(spaceSpan);
      }
    });
  }

  function cycle() {
    const letters = container.querySelectorAll(".letter");
    letters.forEach((l, i) => {
      l.style.animationDelay = `${i * 0.015}s`;
      l.classList.add("out");
    });

    setTimeout(() => {
      index = (index + 1) % words.length;
      if (index === 0 && !hasLoopedOnce) {
        hasLoopedOnce = true;
        words = looped;
      }
      setWord(index);
      setTimeout(cycle, words[index].time);
    }, 200);
  }

  // Initial Boot: Wait slightly for the player component to register its methods
  setTimeout(() => {
    setWord(index);
    setTimeout(cycle, words[index].time);
  }, 500);
});
</script>
